\displaystyle+\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(6)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{+}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n^{\prime},+,l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{+}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n^{\prime},+,l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle

(7)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{-}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n^{\prime},-,l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{-}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n^{\prime},-,l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

(8)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{+}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n^{\prime},+,l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{+}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n^{\prime},+,l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

(9)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{-}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n^{\prime},-,l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{-}}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n^{\prime},-,l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

(10)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{n,j,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\beta_{n,j,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},l}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(t)\top}\right),

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

Appendix F
Concentration Inequalities

In this section, we will give some concentration inequalities that show some important properties of the data and the ViT parameters at random initialization.

Lemma F.1

(Lemma B.1 in
6
)
.

Suppose that
\delta>0
and
n\geq 8\log(4/\delta)
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\frac{N}{4}\leq|\{n\in[N]:y_{n}=1\}|,|\{n\in[N]:y_{n}=-1\}|\leq\frac{3N}{4}.

Lemma F.2

(Initialization of V, Lemma C.2 in
34
)
.

Suppose that
\delta>0
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

|V_{\pm}^{(0)}|\leq d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}},\quad|V_{n,i}^{(0)}|\leq d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

Lemma F.3

(Initialization of QK, Lemma C.3 in
34
)
.

Suppose that
\delta>0
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d_{h}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d_{h}}{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}}{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d_{h}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d_{h}}{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}}{2},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}\sigma_{h}^{2}d^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\mp}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}\sigma_{h}^{2}d^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}\sigma_{h}^{2}d^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}\sigma_{h}^{2}d^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(0)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\}\text{ and }n,n^{\prime}\in[N]
.

Lemma F.4

(Lemma B.2 in
6
and Lemma B.4 in
35
)
.

Suppose that
\delta>0
and
d=\Omega(\log(4NM/\delta))
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\xi}_{n,i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2},

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}_{n,i},\boldsymbol{\xi}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)},

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}-2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}-\tau^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2}+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2},

\displaystyle(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\pm},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}^{\prime}_{\pm}\rangle\leq(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2},

\displaystyle|\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\pm},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2}

\displaystyle|\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2}

for
i,i^{\prime}\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq i^{\prime}\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.

Appendix G
Benign Overfitting in Case 1

In this section, we consider the benign overfitting regime under the condition that
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}=\Omega(1)
and
\tau\leq O(\tfrac{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}}{\log d_{h}})
. We analyze the dynamics of
V_{\pm}
,
V_{n,i}
, the inner product
\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}
,
\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}
, and
\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}
,
\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}
during adversarial training, and further give the upper bound for clean test error and robust test error. The proofs in this section are based on the results in Section
F
, which hold with high probability.

G.1
Stage i

In Stage I,
V_{\pm}^{(t)}
,
V_{n,i}^{(t)}
begin to pull apart until
|V_{\pm}^{(t)}|
is sufficiently larger than
|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|
. At the same time, the inner products of
q
and
k
maintain their magnitude.

Lemma G.1

(Gradient of Loss)
.

As long as
\max\{|V_{+}^{(t)}|,|V_{-}^{(t)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|\}=o(1)
, we have
-\ell^{\prime}(y_{n}f(\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))
remains
1/2\pm o(1)
.

Proof.

Note that
\ell(z)=\log(1+\exp(-z))
and
-\ell^{\prime}=\exp(-z)/(1+\exp(-z))
, without loss of generality, we assume
y_{n}=1
, we have

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(t)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(t)\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n})\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}.

Note that

\displaystyle-\max\{|V_{+}^{(t)}|,|V_{-}^{(t)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|\}\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(t)\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n}^{\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\leq\max\{|V_{+}^{(t)}|,|V_{-}^{(t)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\mathbf{\widetilde{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1+\exp(0+o(1))}\geq\frac{1}{2+o(1)}\geq\frac{1}{2}-o(1),

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\mathbf{\widetilde{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(0+o(1))}{1+\exp(0+o(1))}\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{1+1+o(1)}\leq\frac{1}{2}+o(1).

∎

Lemma G.2

(Bound of Attention)
.

As long as
|\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1).

Proof.

It is clear that
\exp(o(1))=1+o(1)
. Therefore, as long as
|\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+(M-1)+(M-1)o(1)}=\frac{1}{1+(M-1)\exp(o(1))}=

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp(-o(1))}{\exp(-o(1))+(M-1)\exp(o(1))}\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{\exp(o(1))}{\exp(o(1))+(M-1)\exp(-o(1))}

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp(o(1))}{\exp(o(1))+(M-1)}=\frac{1+o(1)}{1+o(1)+(M-1)}=\frac{1}{M}+o(1)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}-o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\mathbf{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1).

∎

Lemma G.3

(Upper bound of V)
.

Let
T_{0}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
. Then under the same conditions as Theorem
4.2
we have

|V_{+}^{(t)}|,|V_{-}^{(t)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|=\mathcal{O}(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})

for
t\in[0,T_{0}]
.

Proof.

By Lemma
E.4
, we have

\displaystyle|\gamma_{V,+}^{(t+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\bigg(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{NM}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}(1+(M-1))+\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{NM}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}M(M-1)

\displaystyle\leq O(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}),

where the second inequality is by Lemma
F.1
and Lemma
F.4
and
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\leq 1
.

Similarly, we have

|\gamma_{V,-}^{(t+1)}-\gamma_{V,-}^{(t)}|\leq O(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}).

By Definition
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle|V_{+}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle=|V_{+}^{(0)}+\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}(\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq|V_{+}^{(0)}|+\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}|\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)}|\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}+O(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2})\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality is by Lemma
F.2
. Similarly, we have
|V_{-}^{(t)}|=O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})
.
By Lemma
E.4
, we have

\displaystyle|\rho_{V,n,i}^{(t+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|-\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n^{\prime}}^{\prime(t)}\left(\left(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n^{\prime},k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n^{\prime}\in S_{-}}\left(\cdot\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{2NM}\cdot M+\frac{\eta}{NM}\cdot MN\cdot 2\sigma_{p}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+(2M-1)\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{3d/2}+M\tau^{2})}{NM}\cdot NM

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}+\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4MN/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{NM}\cdot NM^{2}

\displaystyle=O(\eta(\max\{(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2},\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}\}))

\displaystyle=O(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2})

where the second inequality is by Lemma
F.4
and
-\ell_{n}^{\prime(t)}\leq 1
, the third inequality is by
d=\widetilde{\Omega}(\epsilon^{-2}N^{2}d_{h})\geq 4N\sqrt{\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}
, the last inequality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}=\Omega(1)
. Then by Definition
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle=|V_{n,i}^{(0)}+\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}(\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s)})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq|V_{n,i}^{(0)}|+\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}|\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}+O(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2})\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality is by Lemma C.2, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma G.4

(Inner Products Hold Magnitude)
.

Let
T_{0}=O\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
. Then under the same conditions as Theorem
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h})

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}
,
n,n^{\prime}\in[N]
and
t\in[0,T_{0}]
.

The proof for Lemma
G.4
is in Section
J.4
. Note that
\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}}
, thus
O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)=o(1)
.

Lemma G.5

(V’s Beginning of Learning Signals)
.

Under the same conditions as Theorem
4.2
, there exists

T_{1}=\frac{10M(3M+1)N}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(N(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}-(N+30M^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})-60M^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d\right)\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}}

such that the first element of the vector
\widetilde{X}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}
dominates its other elements, that is,

V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,\quad\text{for all }n\in S_{+},\;i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},

V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,\quad\text{for all }n\in S_{-},\;i\in[M]\setminus\{1\}.

Proof.

Let
C
be a constant larger than
10M(3M+1)
. As long as

N(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}-(N+30M^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})-60M^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d\geq\frac{10M(3M+1)}{C}N(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}.

Thus, we further get

T_{1}=\frac{10M(3M+1)N}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(N(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}-(N+30M^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})-60M^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d\right)\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}}

\leq\frac{C}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}}\right),

which satisfies the time condition in Lemma
G.3
and Lemma
G.4
. Then by Lemma
G.1
and Lemma
G.2
we have:

-\ell_{n}^{\prime(t)}=\frac{1}{2}\pm o(1),

\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1),

\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\leq\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1).

For
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\}
,
n\in[N]
, and
t\in[0,T_{1}]
, plugging these into the update rule for
\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(t)}
shown in Lemma
E.4
, we have:

\displaystyle\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(t+1)}-\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\bigg(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}}{NM}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}\pm o(1)\right)\cdot M\left(\frac{1}{M}\pm o(1)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{NM}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}\pm o(1)\right)\cdot M(M-1)\left(\frac{1}{M}\pm o(1)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}-(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)}{10M}

Then by Definition
E.1
and summing over
T_{1}
steps, we have:

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(T_{1})}

\displaystyle\geq-|V_{+}^{(0)}|+T_{1}\cdot\frac{\eta\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}-(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)}{10M}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(11)

\displaystyle=-d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}+T_{1}\cdot\frac{\eta\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}-(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)}{10M}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

Similarly, we have:

V_{-}^{(T_{1})}\leq d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}-T_{1}\cdot\frac{\eta\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}-(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)}{10M}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(12)

Similarly, by the bound

|\rho_{V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}}-\rho_{V_{n,i}^{(t)}}|\leq\frac{2\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}+\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})

given in equation (
G.1
), we have

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|

\displaystyle\leq|V_{n,i}^{(0)}|+T_{1}\cdot\left(\frac{2\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}+\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(13)

\displaystyle=d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}+T_{1}\cdot\left(\frac{2\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}+\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+2\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

According to equations (
11
), (
12
), and (
13
), it is easy to verify that

V_{+}^{(T_{1})}-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|\geq 0\quad\text{and}\quad V_{-}^{(T_{1})}+3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|\leq 0,

which completes the proof.

∎

G.2
Stage ii

In stage II,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle,\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle
grows while
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}\rangle,\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}\rangle
decreases, resulting in attention focusing more and more on the signals and less on the noises. By the results of stage I, we have the following conditions at the beginning of stage II

V_{+}^{(T_{1})}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|,

V_{-}^{(T_{1})}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|,

|V_{+}^{(T_{1})}|,|V_{-}^{(T_{1})}|,|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|=O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{\mp}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h})

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N]
.

Notations. To better characterize the gap between different inner products, we define the following notations:

•

denote
\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(t)}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle,\quad n\in S_{+}
.

•

denote
\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(t)}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle,\quad n\in S_{-}
.

•

denote
\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(t)}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle,\quad n\in S_{+}
.

•

denote
\Lambda_{n,i,-,j}^{(t)}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle,\quad n\in S_{-}
.

Lemma G.6

(Upper bound of V)
.

Let
T_{0}=O\left(\frac{1}{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)
. Then under the same conditions as Theorem
4.2
, we have

|V_{+}^{(t)}|,|V_{-}^{(t)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|=o(1)

for
t\in[0,T_{0}]
.

The proof of Lemma
G.6
is similar to that of Lemma
G.3
, except that the time
T_{0}
is changed. Let
T_{2}=\Omega\left(\frac{1}{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)
, then by Lemma
G.6
and Lemma
G.1
we have
\frac{1}{2}-o(1)\leq-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\leq\frac{1}{2}+o(1)
for
n\in[N],t\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
, which can simplify the calculations of
\alpha
and
\beta
defined in Definition
E.3
by their bounds. Next we prove the following four propositions
\mathcal{B}(t),\mathcal{C}(t),\mathcal{D}(t),\mathcal{E}(t)
by induction on
t
for
t\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
:

•

\mathcal{B}(t)
:

V_{+}^{(t)}\geq\eta C_{3}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1})

V_{-}\leq\eta C_{3}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1})

V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

|V_{+}^{(t)}|\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1})

|V_{-}^{(t)}|\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1})

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

•

\mathcal{C}(t)
:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}ddd_{h}),

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

\text{for }i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}.

•

\mathcal{D}(t)
:

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle\geq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle\geq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(t+1)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta\right))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1)\right)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\log\Bigg(\exp\big(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}\,\big(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2}\big)\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}}{N\big(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\big)^{2}}\cdot\ (t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1)\Bigg).

\text{for }i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N].

•

\mathcal{E}(t)
:

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|\leq\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}})

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)

\text{for }i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,\bar{n}\in[N],n\neq\bar{n}.

By the results of Stage I, we know that
\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\mathcal{C}(T_{1}),\mathcal{E}(T_{1})
are true. To prove that
\mathcal{B}(t),\mathcal{C}(t),\mathcal{D}(t)
and
\mathcal{E}(t)
are true in Stage II, we will prove the following claims holds for
t\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
:

Claim 1
.

\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(t-1),\mathcal{E}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{E}(t)\implies\mathcal{B}(t+1)

Claim 2
.

\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{B}(t),\mathcal{C}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{C}(t),\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(t-1){,\mathcal{E}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{E}(t)}\implies\mathcal{D}(t)

Claim 3
.

\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{B}(t),\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(t-1),\mathcal{E}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{E}(t)\implies\mathcal{C}(t+1)

Claim 4
.

\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{B}(t),\mathcal{C}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{C}(t),\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(t-1),\mathcal{E}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{E}(t)\implies\mathcal{E}(t+1)

First, we emphasize that when the perturbation is sufficiently small,
the softmax of the inner product
\langle\mathbf{q},\mathbf{k}\rangle
remains robust
with respect to such perturbations. This is formally established in
Lemma
5.3
. Importantly, this lemma enables us to uniformly control
the behavior of the softmax function, rather than being restricted to the
specific case of
\widetilde{q}^{(t)}
and
\widetilde{k}^{(t)}
at
iteration
t
.

Lemma G.7
.

Suppose the perturbation satisfies
\tau\leq O(\tfrac{\|\boldsymbol{\boldsymbol{\mu}}\|_{2}}{\log d_{h}})
and
t\geq\Omega\left(\frac{1}{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)
(
\mathcal{E}(t)
holds). Then there exists a universal constant
C\leq e/2
such that

{\max\limits_{\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)}{softmax}\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)}/{\min\limits_{\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)}{softmax}\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)}\;\leq\;C.

Proof.

For any perturbed query-key pair, the softmax weight can be written as

\displaystyle softmax\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp\!\left(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)}{\exp\!\left(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp\!\left(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle\right)}

(14)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\exp\!\big(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\big)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp\!\big(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\big)}.

Next, we analyze the difference in the logits. Expanding the perturbation terms yields

\displaystyle\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

(15)

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}-\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}\rangle+\langle\tau_{+}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}-\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\tau_{+}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(t)}-\tau_{n,j}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(t)}\rangle+\langle\tau_{+}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(t)},\tau_{+}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(t)}-\tau_{n,j}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}-\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\;\pm o(1).

The first equality follows from decomposing the perturbed terms, while the second uses the perturbation bound

\tau\leq O(\tfrac{\|\boldsymbol{\boldsymbol{\mu}}\|_{2}}{\log d_{h}})
, together with the assumption that the logit magnitudes satisfy

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,\;|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,\;|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,\;|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|\;\leq\;\log\!\left(d_{h}^{1/2}\right).

An analogous relation holds for differences involving
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(t)}
as well.

Substituting (
15
) into (
14
), we obtain

\frac{1}{C}\,softmax\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)\;\leq\;softmax\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right)\;\leq\;C\,softmax\!\left(\langle\mathbf{q}_{+}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle\right),

for some absolute constant
1\leq C\leq e/2
. This establishes the claim.

Thus, in the following discussion, we show that the updates related to
\boldsymbol{q}^{(t)}
and
\boldsymbol{k}^{(t)}
during adversarial training can be bounded, while the effect of the perturbation does not accumulate over time. Since
C
is a very small function, when computing the single-step update, we approximate the perturbed softmax at the previous time step by its clean state.
∎

G.2.1
Proof of Claim
1

By the results of Stage I, we have

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|=o(1)

Assume that
\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(t-1)
(
t\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
) are true, then
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically non-decreasing and
\langle q_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically non-increasing for
s\in[T_{1},t-1]
, so we have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle,\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle\geq-o(1),

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle,\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle\leq o(1),

for
s\in[T_{1},t]
. Further we have the lower bounds for the attention on signal
\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+}\text{ as follows for }s\in[T_{1},t]
:

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\exp(-o(1))}{\exp(-o(1))+(M-1)\exp(o(1))}

(16)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+(M-1)\exp(o(1))}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+(M-1)+(M-1)o(1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}-o(1),

where the second equality is by
\exp(o(1))=1+o(1)
. Similarly, we have

\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\frac{1}{M}-o(1).

(17)

Plugging them in the update rule for
\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s)}
shown in Lemma
E.4
and we have

\displaystyle\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\bigg(\frac{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}}{NMC}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}\pm o(1)\right)\cdot M\left(\frac{1}{M}\pm o(1)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{NMC}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}\pm o(1)\right)\cdot M(M-1)\left(\frac{1}{M}\pm o(1)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}-(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\right)}{10MC}=O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-{\tau})^{2}}{10MC}\right),

for
s\in[T_{1},t]
. The first inequality is by Lemma
5.3
. Then by Definition
E.1
and taking a summation, we have

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\geq V_{+}^{(T_{1})}+(t-T_{1}+1)\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-{\tau})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{10MC}

(18)

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{3}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-{\tau})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1}+1),

where the last inequality is by
V_{+}^{(T_{1})}\geq 0
and
M=\Theta(1)
. Similarly, we have

V_{-}^{(t+1)}\leq-\eta C_{3}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\tau})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1}+1).

(19)

By
|\rho_{V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}}-\rho_{V_{n,i}^{(t)}}|\leq\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2}+\frac{2\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N})
in (
G.1
) and taking a summation, we have

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}|

\displaystyle\leq|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|+(t-T_{1}+1)\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2}+\frac{2\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}.

(20)

Combining (
18
) and (
20
) we have

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(t+1)}-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}|

(21)

\displaystyle\geq V_{+}^{(T_{1})}+(t-T_{1}+1)\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}}{10M}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-3M\cdot(|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|+(t-T_{1}+1)\eta(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\tau^{2},\frac{2C_{p}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}\})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\geq V_{+}^{(T_{1})}-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|+(t-T_{1}+1)\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}}{10M}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-3M\cdot(|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|+(t-T_{1}+1)\eta(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\tau^{2},\frac{2C_{p}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}\})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\geq 0,

where the last inequality is by
V_{+}^{(T_{1})}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|
and requires
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq 60M^{2}C_{p}^{2}
. The proof for
V_{-}^{(t+1)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}|
is the same.

Next, we prove the upper bound for
V_{+}
and
V_{n,i}
. Based on the upper bound of attention(
<1
) and
-\widetilde{\ell}^{\prime}_{n}\leq 1
we have

\displaystyle\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s)}-\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(s)}((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}+\sum_{j=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2})

(22)

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\cdot M

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s)}+\frac{3\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{4}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(s)}+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}

Then we can get that

\displaystyle|V_{+}^{(t+1)}|

\displaystyle\leq V_{+}^{(T_{1})}+(\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(t+1)}-\gamma_{V_{+}}^{(T_{1})})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}

(23)

\displaystyle\leq V_{+}^{(T_{1})}+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1}+1)

where the first inequality is by the monotonicity of
\gamma_{V_{+}}
and the definition of
V_{+}
, the last inequality is by the result of stage 1 where
V_{+}^{(T_{1})}=O(d^{-1})
. Similarly, we have

|V_{-}^{(t+1)}|\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1}+1)

(24)

which completes the proof for the upper bound of
V_{+}
.

Expanding (
20
) yields

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}|

\displaystyle\leq|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|+\eta(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\tau^{2},\frac{2C_{p}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}d}{N}\})\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{1}+1)

(25)

\displaystyle\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}(t-T_{1}+1)

where the last inequality is by the result of phase 1 where
|V_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}|=O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})
and the condition that
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
.

G.2.2
Proof of Claim 2

By the results of
J.6
, we have the dynamic of
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
as follows

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

(26)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})},

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle

(27)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(s)})},

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle

(28)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})},

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle

(29)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\langle

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j})}

(30)

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,-,j})}

(31)

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j})}

(32)

for
s\in[T_{1},t]
. The seven equations above show that
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically increasing and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically decreasing. Next, we provide the logarithmic increasing lower bounds of
\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s+1)}
and
\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s+1)}
.

We have

\displaystyle\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s+1)}-\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)}=(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)-(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)

(33)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{N}(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}+1)\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-T_{1})^{2}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}

where the last inequality is by
\sigma_{h}^{2}\geq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-2}
. Multiply both sides simultaneously by
\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})
and get

\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s+1)}-\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-T_{1})}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}.

(34)

Taking a summation from
T_{1}
to
t
and get

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s+1)}-\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})\geq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-T_{1})}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}

(35)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1).

(36)

By the property that
\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(t)}
is monotonically increasing, we have

\int_{\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(T_{1})}}^{T_{1}}\exp(x)dx\geq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s+1)}-\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1).

(37)

By

\int_{\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(T_{1})}}^{\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(t+1)}}\exp(x)dx=\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(t+1)})-\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(T_{1})})
we get

\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(t+1)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1)\right).

Similarly, we have

\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(t+1)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1)\right).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s+1)}-\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)}=\langle(\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)})-(\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)})\rangle-\langle(\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)})-(\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)})\rangle

(38)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{7}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-T_{1})}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)})}.

Then using the similar method as for
\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(t)}
, we get

\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(t+1)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}}\cdot(t-T_{1})(t-T_{1}+1)\right),

which complete the proof. The proof for Claim 3 is in Section
J.9
.

G.2.3
Proof of Claim 4

By the results of
J.7
, we have

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}

for
s\in[T_{1},t]
. Further we have

\displaystyle\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle+\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}

(39)

\displaystyle=\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)\cdot\exp\left(\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{11}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle).

For the last inequality, by
\eta\leq\widetilde{O}(\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}}
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
and the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
for
s\in[T_{1},t]
, we have
\exp\left(\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\leq\exp(o(1))\leq C_{11}
. Multiplying both sides by
\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right)
simultaneously gives

\displaystyle\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle)\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right)

(40)

\displaystyle\leq C_{11}\exp\!\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right)\cdot\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\eta C_{12}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h},

where the last inequality is by plugging (
39
). Taking a summation, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle}^{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle}\exp(x)\,dx

(41)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle)\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\eta C_{12}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}

\displaystyle\leq T_{2}\cdot\eta C_{12}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{h}^{1/2}}{\log^{2}(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}.

where the first inequality is due to
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
is monotone increasing, the last inequality is by
T_{2}=\Theta(\eta^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{-2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)^{-1})
and
\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}}
. By

\int_{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle}^{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle}\exp(x)dx=\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle)-\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle),

we have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\log\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle+\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)\leq\log\left(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(42)

By the results of
J.7
, we also have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle)}

(43)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle\geq-\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{N}\cdot\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle).

(44)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{N\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)}.

(45)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle\geq-\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{N}\cdot\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle).

(46)

Then using the similar method as for
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle
, we get

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\log\left(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(47)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle\geq-\log\left(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(48)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\log\left(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(49)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle\geq-\log\left(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

(50)

Next we provide the upper bound for
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t+1)}\rangle|
. By the results of
J.8
, we have

\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,i,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,i,-}^{(s)}|=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right),

(51)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|=O\left(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right),

(52)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|=O\left(\text{SNR}\cdot N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)

(53)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},j}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},i}^{(s)}|=O\left(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)

(54)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq n^{\prime}
. Plugging these into the update rule of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle,\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle
and assume that propositions
\mathcal{C}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{C}(t)
hold, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|

(55)

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left(\beta_{-,-}^{(s)\top}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)\top}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)\top}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle+O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(\text{SNR}\cdot N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+o\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)+o\left(\text{SNR}\cdot N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the last equality is by
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|=o(1)
and
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
. Similarly we have
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle|=o(1)
.

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(t+1)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|

(56)

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\beta_{\overline{n},j,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle+\beta_{\overline{n},j,-}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{\overline{n},j,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left(\beta_{\overline{n},j,+}^{(s)\top}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)\top}+\beta_{\overline{n},j,-}^{(s)\top}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)\top}+\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}\beta_{\overline{n},j,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)\top}\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)\top}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,\overline{n},j}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n,l}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\neq\overline{n}\land(l\neq j\lor n^{\prime}\neq\overline{n})}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}|\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{\overline{n},j,+}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{\overline{n},j,-}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{\overline{n},j,n,i}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{\overline{n},j,n,l}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n^{\prime}\neq\overline{n}\land(l\neq i\lor n^{\prime}\neq n)}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\sum_{n^{\prime}=1}^{N}\sum_{l=2}^{M}|\beta_{\overline{n},j,n^{\prime},l}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})\cdot o(1)+O(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))\cdot\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h})

\displaystyle+M\cdot O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle+O(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+o(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})

\displaystyle+O(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}})+o(Nd^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second equality is by

\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}},

the last equality is by
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|=o(1)
,
d=\widetilde{\Omega}(\epsilon^{-2}N^{2}d_{h})
and
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
.

G.3
Stage iii

In Stage III, the outputs of ViT grow up and the loss derivatives are no longer at
o(1)
. We will carefully compute the growth rate of
V_{\pm}
and
V_{n,i}
while keeping monitoring the monotonicity of
\langle q,k\rangle
. By substituting
t=T_{2}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
into propositions
\mathcal{B}(t)
,
\mathcal{C}(t)
,
\mathcal{D}(t)
,
\mathcal{E}(t)
in Stage II, we have the following conditions at the beginning of stage III

\displaystyle|V_{+}^{(T_{2})}|,|V_{-}^{(T_{2})}|

\displaystyle,|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|=o(1),

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(T_{2})}

\displaystyle\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|,

\displaystyle V_{-}^{(T_{2})}

\displaystyle\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}),

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1),

|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1),

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.

\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right)

\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right)

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|\leq\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,\overline{n}\in[N],n\neq\overline{n}
.

Let
T_{3}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
. Next we prove the following four propositions
\mathcal{F}(t)
,
\mathcal{G}(t)
,
\mathcal{H}(t)
,
\mathcal{I}(t)
by induction on
t
for
t\in[T_{2},T_{3}]
:

•

\mathcal{F}(t)
:

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|=o(1),

\displaystyle\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2})\right)\leq V_{+}^{(t)}\leq 2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right),

\displaystyle-2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\leq V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-\log\left(\exp(-V_{-}^{(T_{2})})+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2})\right)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

•

\mathcal{G}(t)
:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.

•

\mathcal{H}(t)
:

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle
