\displaystyle\geq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

•

\mathcal{I}(t)
:

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq\overline{n}
.

By the results of Stage II, we know that
\mathcal{F}(T_{2})
,
\mathcal{G}(T_{2})
,
\mathcal{I}(T_{2})
are true. To prove that
\mathcal{F}(t)
,
\mathcal{G}(t)
,
\mathcal{H}(t)
and
\mathcal{I}(t)
are true in stage 3, we will prove the following claims holds for
t\in[T_{2},T_{3}]
:

•

Claim 5.
\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(t-1),\mathcal{I}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{I}(t)\implies\mathcal{F}(t+1)

•

Claim 6.
\mathcal{F}(t),\mathcal{G}(t),\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(t-1),\mathcal{I}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{I}(t-1)\implies\mathcal{H}(t)

•

Claim 7.
\mathcal{F}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{F}(t),\mathcal{G}(t),\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(t-1),\mathcal{I}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{I}(t)\implies\mathcal{G}(t+1)

•

Claim 8.
\mathcal{F}(T_{2}),..,\mathcal{F}(t),\mathcal{G}(T_{2}),..,\mathcal{G}(t),\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),..,\mathcal{H}(t-1),\mathcal{I}(T_{2}),..,\mathcal{I}(t)\implies\mathcal{I}(t+1)

G.3.1
Proof of Claim 5

The proofs for
V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|
and
V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|
are the same as for
G.2.1
. Based on
\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(t)
where
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically non-decreasing and
\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
,
\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
are monotonically non-increasing for
s\in[T_{2},t-1]
, we have

\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right),

(57)

\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right)

(58)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N],s\in[T_{2},t]
. We further get

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}\leq\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)}=\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)})}

(59)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)}=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right).

For the first inequality, by the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
(
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle
is increasing and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
is decreasing), there exist a constant
C
such that
C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)
. The second inequality is by plugging
57
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}\leq\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(s)})}

(60)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right).

Plugging (
59
) and (
60
) into the update rule of
\rho_{V,n,i}
in Lemma
E.4
and get

\displaystyle|\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s)}|\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}|\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}|\cdot\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle\cdot\left(O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right)

(61)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n^{\prime}\neq n\lor i\neq i^{\prime}}|\widetilde{\ell}_{n^{\prime}}^{{}^{\prime}(t)}|\cdot\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\rangle\cdot\left(O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+o(1))}{2NM}\left(O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{NM}NM(2{\sigma}_{p}^{2}\sqrt{d\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\sqrt{d}+o(1))\cdot\left(O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta}{NM}\left(O\left(\frac{N(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+o(1))(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+o(1))(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

where the second inequality is by Lemma
F.4
and
|\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{(t)}|\leq 1
. For the last inequality, since
d=\widetilde{\Omega}(\epsilon^{-2}N^{2}d_{h})
, we have
N\cdot M\cdot 2{\sigma}_{p}^{2}\sqrt{d\log(4N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\leq\frac{1}{2}{\sigma}_{p}^{2}d
. By Definition
E.1
and taking a summation we have

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(t+1)}|\leq|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|+\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(62)

\displaystyle\leq|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|+T_{3}\cdot|\rho_{V,n,i}^{(t+1)}-\rho_{V,n,i}^{(t)}|\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq o(1)+\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot O\left(\frac{\eta(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+o(1))(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\cdot\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=o(1)+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+o(1))(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=o(1)+o(1)

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the first equality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
,
the second equality is by

d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\!\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
.
Then we have a constant upper bound for the sum of
V_{n,i}
as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=(M-1)\cdot o(1)\leq C_{15},

for
n\in[N],s\in[T_{2},t]
.

Expanding (
59
) and (
60
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}=O\!\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{3/2}}\right)=o(1),

(63)

where the equality is by

d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\!\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}=O\!\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}d\,d_{h}^{1/2}}\right)=o(1),

(64)

where the equality is by the same choice of
d_{h}
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)

\displaystyle\geq 1-(M-1)\cdot o(1)\geq 1-o(1),

(65)

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)

\displaystyle\geq 1-(M-1)\cdot o(1)\geq 1-o(1),

(66)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N],s\in[T_{2},t]
.

Thus, in the following discussion, we omit the noise-related terms as
o(1)
.

Next we provide the bounds for
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}
.
Note that
\ell(z)=\log(1+\exp(-z))
and
-\ell^{\prime}(z)=\exp(-z)/(1+\exp(-z))
.
Without loss of generality, assume
y_{n}=1
. We have

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\!\left(\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{(s)\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}^{(s)})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(67)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\Big(\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\Big)\cdot\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}\Big(\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,l}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)\Big)\cdot\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,j}^{(s)\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\Big(M\cdot(1-o(1))\cdot V_{+}^{(s)}+M\cdot o(1)\cdot\sum_{j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}V_{n,i}^{(s)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{1}{2}V_{+}^{(s)},

for
s\in[T_{2},t]
,
where the second equality is by plugging equations above, and the inequality follows from
V_{+}^{(s)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|
.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{(s)\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}^{(s)})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}\{V_{+}^{(s)},V_{n,i}^{(s)}\}=V_{+}^{(s)}.

(68)

Then we have

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\widetilde{X}_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\!\left(\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{x}_{n,l}^{(s)\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{X}_{n}^{(s)})^{\top})\widetilde{X}_{n}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(69)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

where the first inequality is by plugging (
68
). For the last inequality, note that
V_{+}^{(T_{2})}\geq 0
and
V_{+}^{(s)}
is monotonically increasing, so there exist a constant
C_{16}
such that
\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}\geq\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}
. We also have the upper bound

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(\boldsymbol{X}_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\boldsymbol{x}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{n})^{\top})\boldsymbol{X}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(70)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)}

Then by the update rule of
\gamma_{V,+}^{(t)}
and in Lemma
E.4
and get

\displaystyle\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.

(71)

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle\geq-\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(M\cdot(1-o(1)))

\displaystyle\geq\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot(1-o(1))\cdot\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}\frac{1}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

where the second inequality is by (
69
). Then by definition
E.1
, we get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=(\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

(72)

Multiply both sides simultaneously by
\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})
and get

\displaystyle\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(73)

Taking a summation from
T_{2}
to
t
and get

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(74)

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)

By the property that
V_{+}^{(s)}
is monotonically increasing, we have

\displaystyle\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}}\exp(x)dx

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

(75)

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)

By
\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}/2}\exp(x)dx=\exp(V_{+}^{(t+1)}/2)-\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2)
we get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2)+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)\right)

(76)

\displaystyle\geq\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle V_{-}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\leq-\log\left(\exp\left(V_{-}^{(T_{2})}\right)+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)\right)

(77)

Next we provide upper bounds for
V_{+}^{(t+1)}
and
V_{-}^{(t+1)}
. By the update rule of
\gamma_{V,+}^{(t)}
and in Lemma
E.4
we have

\displaystyle\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}\Bigg(\frac{\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\big)}{\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\big)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle\big)}

(78)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\big)}{\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\big)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,j}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle\big)}\Bigg)+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\big(-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}\big)\cdot M

\displaystyle\leq\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}\cdot\exp\!\big(-V_{+}^{(s)}/2\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{4}\,\exp\!\big(-V_{+}^{(s)}/2\big).

where the second inequality is by (
70
). Then by definition
E.1
, we get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=(\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(79)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{4\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)}

Further we have

\displaystyle\exp(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2)

\displaystyle\leq\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2+\frac{3\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)}

(80)

\displaystyle=\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)\cdot\exp(\frac{3\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)})

\displaystyle\leq C_{18}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)

For the last inequality, by
\eta\leq\widetilde{O}(\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
V_{+}^{(T_{2})}=\Theta(1)
and the monotonicity of
V_{+}^{(s)}
, we have
\exp(\frac{3\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)})\leq C_{18}
. Multiplying both sides by
(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2-V_{+}^{(s)}/2)
simultaneously gives

\displaystyle\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2-V_{+}^{(s)}/2)

\displaystyle\leq C_{18}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2)(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2-V_{+}^{(s)}/2)

(81)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta C_{18}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}

where the last inequality is by plugging (
80
). Taking a summation we have

\displaystyle\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}/2}\exp(x)dx

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{T_{3}}\exp(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2)(V_{+}^{(s+1)}/2-V_{+}^{(s)}/2)

(82)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{T_{3}}\frac{3\eta C_{18}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{3\eta C_{18}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}=O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)

By
\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}/2}\exp(x)dx=\exp(V_{+}^{(t+1)}/2)-\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2)
we have

V_{+}^{(t+1)}\leq 2\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})}/2)+O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)=2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

Similarly, we have

V_{-}^{(t+1)}\geq-2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

G.3.2
Proof of Claim 6

By
\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(t-1)
, we have
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle),\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle)=1-o(1)
and
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle),\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)=o(1)
, which have been proved in
G.3.1
. By the results of
J.5
, we have the signs of
\alpha
and
\beta
as follows:

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)},\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)},\beta_{+,+}^{(t)},\beta_{-,-}^{(t)},\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)},\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)},\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)},\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq 0,

\displaystyle\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)},\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(t)},\alpha_{n,i,n,j}^{(t)},\beta_{n,i,+}^{(t)},\beta_{n,i,-}^{(t)},\beta_{n,j,n,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq 0.

Then combined with
\mathcal{G}(T)
and we have the dynamics of
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
as follows:

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

(83)

\displaystyle+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\{\text{lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\geq 0

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq 0,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq,0

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 0,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 0

which completes the proof. The proof for Claim 7 is in Section
J.12

G.3.3
Proof of Claim 8

By the results of
J.10
, we have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\!\left(\tfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp\!\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)},

(84)

Further we have

\displaystyle\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle)\leq\exp\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle+\frac{\eta C_{10}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)\cdot\exp\left(\frac{\eta C_{10}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{11}\exp\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right).

For the last inequality, by
\eta\leq\widetilde{O}(\min\{\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}}
,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
and the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
for
s\in[T_{1},t]
, we have
\exp\left(\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)\leq\exp(o(1))\leq C_{11}
. Multiplying both sides by
\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)
simultaneously gives

\displaystyle\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle)\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)

(85)

\displaystyle\leq C_{11}\exp\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)\cdot\left(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\eta C_{12}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right),

where the last inequality is by plugging (
84
). Taking a summation we have

\displaystyle\int_{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle}^{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle}\exp(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\exp\!\big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle\big)\Big(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\Big)

(86)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\eta C_{12}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\!\left(O\!\left(\tfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq T_{3}\cdot\eta C_{12}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\!\left(O\!\left(\tfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\!\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\tfrac{1}{2}}\log\!\left(O\!\left(\tfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{\tfrac{3}{2}}}\right).

where the first inequality is due to
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
is monotone increasing, the last equality is by
T_{3}=\Theta(\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{-1}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{-2})
,
\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(1)
and
\sigma_{h}^{2}\leq\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-\frac{3}{2}}
. By
\int_{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle}^{\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle}\exp(x)dx=\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle)-\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle)
, we have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle\leq\log\left(\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle)+O\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\right)\leq\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})

where the last inequality is by
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle\leq\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})
. By the results of
J.10
, we also have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle q-^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle)}.

(87)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle\geq-\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{N}\cdot\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle).

(88)

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{N\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)}.

(89)

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle\geq-

\displaystyle\frac{\eta C_{10}\sigma_{p}^{2}d(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{N}

(90)

\displaystyle\cdot\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle).

Then using the similar method as for
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle
, we get

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

(91)

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

Next we provide the upper bound for
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t+1)}\rangle|
. By the results of
J.11
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\text{SNR}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right),

(92)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,i,-}^{(t)}|

(93)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right),

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n,j}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,j,n,i}^{(t)}|

(94)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right),

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in S_{\pm}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}|,\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},i}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{4}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

(95)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq n^{\prime}
. Plugging these and proposition
\mathcal{G}(t)
into the update rule of
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\bar{n},j}^{(t)}\rangle|
and get

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle|\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|

(96)

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)\top}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(\frac{\text{SNR}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(\frac{\text{SNR}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|+o\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)+o\left(\frac{N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality is by results in
E.2
, the last equality is by
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1)
and
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
. Similarly we have
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle|=o(1)
and
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\bar{n},j}^{(t+1)}\rangle|=o(1)
.

Lemma G.8

(Convergence of Training Loss, Lemma D.7 in
(
34
)
)
.

There exist
T=\frac{C_{19}}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}
such that

L_{S}(\theta(T))\leq\epsilon

(97)

Proof.

As we have the same conditions at the end of stage III as
(
34
)
, thus we have:
Substituting
t=T=\frac{C_{19}}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}
into propositions
\mathcal{F}(t)
and get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})+\frac{C_{20}}{\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\frac{C_{20}}{\epsilon}\right),

|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|=O(1).

Thus, for
n\in S_{+}
, we bound
f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(t))
as follows :

f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta)=\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\geq\log\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)

(98)

And

\displaystyle\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\log\left(1+\exp(-f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(t)))

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\log\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon.

Similarly, we have
\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{(t)}\leq\epsilon
for
n\in S_{-}
. Therefore, we have

L_{S}(\theta(T))=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{(t)}\leq\epsilon.

∎

G.4
Test error

In this section, we denote clean
V_{+},V_{-}
and
V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}}
as
\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}},\boldsymbol{\mu}_{-}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}
and
\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}
, and perturbed
\widetilde{V}_{+},\widetilde{V}_{-}
and
\widetilde{V}_{\boldsymbol{\xi}}
as
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{-}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}
and
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}
.

G.4.1
Clean test error

Theorem G.9
.

Under Assumption
4.1
, in the theoretical analysis of test error in the second and third stages of benign overfitting, we define
g(\boldsymbol{\xi})
as

V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(t)}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\rangle
. Then, we know that for any
x\geq 0
, if
g:\mathbb{R}^{n}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is a Lipschitz function and
c
is a constant, the following inequality holds for the test loss.

\mathbb{P}(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{i}(g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j})-\mathbb{E}g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}))\geq x)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cx^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2})\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right).

Proof.

According to Theorem 5.2.2 in
50
, we know that for any
x\geq 0
, if
g:\mathbb{R}^{n}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is a Lipschitz function, it holds that

\mathbb{P}(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha_{i}(g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{i})-\mathbb{E}g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{i}))\geq x)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{cx^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha_{i}^{2})\|g\|_{\text{Lip}}^{2}}\right)

(99)

where
g(\boldsymbol{\xi})
is defined as
|V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(t)}|=|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle|
, we have

\displaystyle|g(\boldsymbol{\xi})-g(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle\right|-\left|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\prime},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle\right|\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}-\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\prime},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{\xi}-\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\prime}\|_{2}

So, we can get

\|g\|_{\text{Lip}}\leq\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}.

(100)

By plugging (
100
) into (
99
), we can get the result.
∎

The following inequality holds according to the update rules of the
V
vector in Lemma
E.4
, the first equality is derived from the update of triangle inequality, and the second equality is due to the initialization of the
V
vector.

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(0)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t^{\prime}+1)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}-\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t^{\prime})}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}

(101)

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(0)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}+tO\left(\eta\cdot\max\left\{\left\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\right\|_{2},\sigma_{p}\sqrt{d}\right\}\cdot\left\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\sigma_{V}\left\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}\sqrt{d}+t\eta\left\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|^{2}\max\left\{\left\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\right\|_{2},\sigma_{p}\sqrt{d}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(t\eta\left\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|^{2}\max\left\{\left\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\right\|_{2},\sigma_{p}\sqrt{d}\right\}\right)

Since
g(\boldsymbol{\xi})
as
|V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(t)}|=|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle|
, and since
\left\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\rangle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2})
, so we can get:

\mathbb{E}g(\boldsymbol{\xi})=\mathbb{E}|\langle\boldsymbol{\xi},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle|=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}

The test error can be interpreted as the probability that the noise term dominates the signal term.
Formally, this corresponds to the event that the cumulative contribution of the random perturbation
exceeds the deterministic signal margin.
After centralization, we can apply Theorem
G.9
to obtain a high-probability upper bound on the test error.

\displaystyle P(y(f(\theta,\mathbf{X}))\leq 0)

\displaystyle=P\left(\left[(\sum_{i=1}^{M}S_{i,1})(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)})+\sum_{j=2}^{M}((\sum_{i=1}^{M}S_{i,j})V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)})\right]\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sum_{j=2}^{M}((\sum_{i=1}^{M}S_{i,j})|V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)}|)\geq(\sum_{i=1}^{M}S_{i,1})\left(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}(g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j})-E\{g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j})\}\geq\alpha_{1}\left(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\right)-\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\frac{2}{{\pi}}}(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j})\ \left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left[-\frac{c_{2}\left(\alpha_{1}\left(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\right)-\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\frac{2}{{\pi}}}(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j})\ \left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}\right)^{2}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2})\left(\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\frac{c_{4}}{\pi}\right)\cdot\exp\left[-\frac{c_{5}}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}\left(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\right)}{\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{-V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

We denote
\alpha_{j}
as
\sum_{i}S_{i,j}
, where
S_{i,j}
is
softmax(\langle\mathbf{q}_{i}^{(t)},\mathbf{k}_{j}^{(t)}\rangle)
. When the subscript is 1, it represents the signal, and when the subscript is from 2 to M, it represents noise.

Then, by the lower bound of
V_{\pm}^{(t)}
and upper bound of
\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}
, we can further bound the test error with following inequality:

\displaystyle P(y(f(\theta,\mathbf{X})\leq 0)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\frac{c_{4}}{\pi}\right)\cdot\exp\left[-\frac{c_{5}}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}\left(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\right)}{\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\right\|_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\frac{c_{12}}{\pi}\right)\exp\left[-\frac{c_{13}}{2}O\left(\frac{V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}}{\frac{\sigma_{p}(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)})}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\frac{c_{12}}{\pi}\right)\exp\left[-\frac{c_{13}}{2}O\left(dSNR^{2}\right)\right]

where the second inequality is by
\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}
is almost aligned with
V_{+}^{(t)}
and
V_{-}^{(t)}
, thus
\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}\sim\frac{V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}}
.

G.4.2
Robust test error

We start by writing the prediction score under perturbation
\widetilde{X}
as

\displaystyle yf(\theta,\widetilde{X})=\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{M}\widetilde{S}_{i1}\Big)\big(\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{V}_{-}^{(t)}\big)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{M}\widetilde{S}_{ij}\Big)\widetilde{V}_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)},

We first take the first term as an example and derive an upper bound on the maximum discrepancy between the perturbed input
\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)
and the clean input.

\displaystyle S_{11}V_{+}^{(t)}-\widetilde{S}_{11}\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=({S}_{11}-\widetilde{S}_{11})V_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{S}_{11}(V_{+}^{(t)}-\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\leq(1-1/C)S_{11}{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{S}_{11}|\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+},\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq(1-1/C)S_{11}{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{S}_{11}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\big\|\tau,

where the inequality comes from Lemma
G.7
, and the definition of
V_{+}^{(t)}
. This shows that the deviation can be bounded linearly in the perturbation magnitude, with only a small residual term since
(C-1)=o(1)
.
Similarly, an analogous upper bound holds for
\widetilde{S}_{i1}\widetilde{V}_{\pm}^{(t)}-S_{i1}V_{\pm}^{(t)}
and
\widetilde{S}_{ij}\widetilde{V}_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)}-S_{ij}V_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)}
for
i\in[M],\,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}
.

Aggregating the deviations across all components, the worst-case perturbation satisfies

\displaystyle yf(\theta,X)-\min_{\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)}yf(\theta,\widetilde{X})

(102)

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\sum_{i}{S}_{i1}\Big)\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|\tau+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\Big(\sum_{i}{S}_{ij}\Big)\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|\tau+(1-1/C)S_{11}(\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)})+o(1)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{M}\sum_{i}{S}_{ij}\Bigg)\big\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\big\|\tau+(1-1/C)\Big(\sum_{i}{S}_{i1}\Big)(\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{V}_{-}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=M\big\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\big\|\tau+(1-1/C)\Big(\sum_{i}{S}_{i1}\Big)(\widetilde{V}_{+}^{(t)}+\widetilde{V}_{-}^{(t)})

where the first inequality comes from
\widetilde{V}_{\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j}}^{(t)}=o(1)
for
j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}
. Thus the adversarial effect scales with both the cumulative magnitude of the perturbed coefficients and the operator norm of the weight matrices. Then we can bound the robust test error:

\displaystyle P

\displaystyle\Bigg(\min_{\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)}yf(\theta,\widetilde{X})\leq 0\Bigg)=P\Bigg(yf(\theta,X)+\big(yf(\theta,X)-\min_{\widetilde{X}\in B(X,\tau)}yf(\theta,\widetilde{X})\big)\leq 0\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq P\Bigg(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}\big(g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j})-E\{g(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{j})\}\big)\geq\alpha_{1}\big(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)}\big)-\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{\pi}}\Big(\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}\Big)\big\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-M\big\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\big\|_{2}\tau-(1-1/C)\alpha_{1}({V}_{+}^{(t)}-{V}_{-}^{(t)})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\!\left(\tfrac{c_{12}}{\pi}\right)\exp\!\left[-\tfrac{c_{13}}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}(V_{+}^{(t)}-V_{-}^{(t)})}{\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2}}\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}}-\frac{M\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|\tau}{\sigma_{p}\sqrt{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\alpha_{j}^{2}}\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\!\left(\tfrac{c_{12}}{\pi}\right)\exp\!\left[-\tfrac{c_{13}}{2}\,O\!\left(\sqrt{d}SNR(1-\frac{\tau}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}})\right)^{2}\right]

The first inequality follows from (
102
).
The third inequality uses the fact that
C\leq e/2
, which we absorb into the constant term.
The last inequality follows from the bound on
V_{\pm}^{(t)}
and
\|\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(t)}{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}
.

This completes the proof.

Appendix H
Benign Overfitting in Case 2

Stage I stay same with
G.1
. Next, we aim to prove that under condition
\tau=(1-o(1))\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|
, the attention component in a ViT will remain in its initialization state and fail to learn meaningful signal-to-signal or noise-to-signal interactions. This is because, under such perturbations, any newly emerging margin can be immediately neutralized, preventing the signal-to-signal attention from accumulating advantages. In this regime, the ViT effectively degenerates into a linear model.

At the end of Stage I, since
V_{+}^{T_{1}}\geq 3M|V_{n,i}^{T_{1}}|
and
q=o(1)
at this point, the perturbation has no significant effect. Consequently,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle
and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle
experiences a temporary increase, but it does not exceed
\Theta(\log C)
(as we will demonstrate later). Therefore, we assume that after Stage II, when
\langle q,k\rangle
has stabilized and the loss derivatives are no longer at the
o(1)
scale. However,
|V_{n,i}^{T_{3}}|
is not
o(1)
; it is of the same order as
|V_{+}^{T_{3}}|
, which implies that a larger SNR is required. The following conditions hold at the beginning of Stage III.

\displaystyle|V_{+}^{(T_{2})}|,|V_{-}^{(T_{2})}|,

\displaystyle|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|=o(1),

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(T_{2})}

\displaystyle\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|,

\displaystyle V_{-}^{(T_{2})}

\displaystyle\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}|,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\log{C}),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\log{C}),

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1),

|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1),

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=\Theta(\log{C})

|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{2})},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(T_{2})}\rangle|=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,\overline{n}\in[N],n\neq\overline{n}
.

Let
T_{3}=\Theta\left(\frac{M}{\eta\epsilon(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
. Next we prove the following four propositions
\mathcal{J}(t)
,
\mathcal{K}(t)
,
\mathcal{L}(t)
by induction on
t
for
t\in[T_{2},T_{3}]
:

•

\mathcal{J}(t)
:

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|=o(1),

\displaystyle\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})+\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2})\right)\leq V_{+}^{(t)}\leq 2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right),

\displaystyle-2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\leq V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-\log\left(\exp(-V_{-}^{(T_{2})})+\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2})\right)

for
i\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N]
.

•

\mathcal{K}(t)
:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(logC),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(logC\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
,
C=O(1)
.

•

\mathcal{L}(t)
:

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=\Theta(\log{C}),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\overline{n},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq\overline{n}
,
C=O(1)
.

By the results of Stage II, we know that
\mathcal{F}(T_{1})
,
\mathcal{G}(T_{2})
,
\mathcal{I}(T_{2})
are true. To prove that
\mathcal{F}(t)
,
\mathcal{G}(t)
,
\mathcal{H}(t)
and
\mathcal{I}(t)
are true in stage III, we will prove the following claims holds for
t\in[T_{2},T_{3}]
:

•

Claim 9.
\mathcal{L}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{L}(t)\implies\mathcal{J}(t+1)

•

Claim 10.
\mathcal{J}(t),\mathcal{L}(t),\mathcal{K}(t)\implies\mathcal{K}(t+1)

•

Claim 11.
\mathcal{J}(t),\mathcal{K}(t),\mathcal{L}(t),\implies\mathcal{L}(t+1)

H.1
Proof of Claim 9

The proofs for
V_{+}^{(t)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|
and
V_{-}^{(t)}\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(t)}|
are the same as for
G.2.1
.

we provide the bounds for
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}
.
Note that
\ell(z)=\log(1+\exp(-z))
and
-\ell^{\prime}(z)=\exp(-z)/(1+\exp(-z))
.
Without loss of generality, assume
y_{n}=1
. We have

\displaystyle-\widetilde{\ell}^{\prime}(f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\!\left(\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(103)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\Big(\text{softmax}(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{\pm}^{(s)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\text{softmax}(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,l}^{(s)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\Big)\cdot\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}\Big(\text{softmax}(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,j}^{(s)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{l=2}^{M}\text{softmax}(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,l}^{(s)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)\Big)\cdot\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,j}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\Big(M\cdot\frac{C}{C+M-1}\cdot V_{+}^{(s)}+M\cdot\frac{1}{C+M-1}\cdot\sum_{j\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}V_{n,i}^{(s)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{1}{2M}V_{+}^{(s)},

for
s\in[T_{2},t]
,
where the second equality is by
\mathcal{L}(t)
that
\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)}=\Theta(\log{C})
and
\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(s)}=\Theta(\log{C})
, and the inequality follows from
V_{+}^{(s)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|
.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[M]\backslash\{1\}}\{V_{+}^{(s)},V_{n,i}^{(s)}\}

(104)

\displaystyle=V_{+}^{(s)}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle-\widetilde{\ell}^{\prime}(f(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\tfrac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{x}}_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n})^{\top})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(105)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

For the last inequality, note that
V_{+}^{(T_{2})}\geq 0
and
V_{+}^{(s)}
is monotonically increasing, so there exist a constant
C_{16}
such that
\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}\geq\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}
. We also have the upper bound

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(f(X_{n},\theta(s)))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{l=1}^{M}\varphi(x_{n,l}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{Q}^{(s)}\mathbf{W}_{K}^{(s)\top}(X_{n})^{\top})X_{n}\mathbf{W}_{V}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\right)}

(106)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1+\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2M)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2M)}

By the update rule of
\gamma_{V,+}^{(t)}
and in Lemma
E.4
and get

\displaystyle\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(t)}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{-\eta\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle}{NM}\bigg(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{k=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{q}}_{n,j}^{(t)},\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}}{NM}\ \sum_{n\in S_{+}}\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(M\cdot\frac{C}{C+M-1})+\frac{\eta\tau\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}}{NM}\ \sum_{n\in S_{+}}\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(M\cdot\frac{1}{C+M-1})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}}{NM}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}-\frac{\eta\tau\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}}{NM}\cdot\frac{N}{4}\cdot\frac{C_{16}}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)}/2M)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}}{M}\frac{1}{\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

where the second inequality is by (
105
)(
106
), the last inequality is by
N\cdot SNR^{2}=\Omega(\frac{1}{\epsilon})
Then by definition
E.1
, we get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=(\gamma_{V,+}^{(s+1)}-\gamma_{V,+}^{(s)})\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{M\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})}

(107)

Multiply both sides simultaneously by
\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})
and get

\displaystyle\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(108)

Taking a summation from
T_{2}
to
t
and get

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}

(109)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)

By the property that
V_{+}^{(s)}
is monotonically increasing, we have

\displaystyle\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}}\exp(x)dx

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\exp(V_{+}^{(s)})(V_{+}^{(s+1)}-V_{+}^{(s)})

(110)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)

By
\int_{V_{+}^{(T_{2})}}^{V_{+}^{(t+1)}}\exp(x)dx=\exp(V_{+}^{(t+1)})-\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})
we get

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\exp(V_{+}^{(T_{2})})+\frac{\eta}{M}C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)\right)

(111)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle V_{-}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\leq-\log\left(\exp\left(V_{-}^{(T_{2})}\right)+\eta C_{17}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(t-T_{2}+1)\right)

(112)

Similarly, we have the lower bound, the proofs are the same as for
G.3.1

H.2
Proof of Claim 10

We first consider the increment of
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
at the
t
-th update when using the original clean data. We then show that, at this step, the effect introduced by the perturbation under adversarial samples dominates the increment learned from the clean data; hence
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
remains stable and bounded.

By the update rule of
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
we have

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(113)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

Subsequently, we establish upper bounds for
\alpha
and
\beta
for clean data.

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(t)}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}

(114)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(t)}\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=2}^{M}(V_{n,i}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(V_{+}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta}{2M}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}V_{+}^{(t)}

Then, we can then compute the incremental growth of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle
after one update step on clean data.

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(115)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\eta}{2M}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}V_{+}^{(t)}\Theta(\log{C})+\frac{3\eta}{2M}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}V_{+}^{(t)}\Theta(\log{C})

Subsequently, we compute at the
t^{th}
iteration the magnitude of the effect that the perturbation imposes on
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle
.

\displaystyle max_{\widetilde{X}^{(t)}\in B({X}^{(t)},\tau)}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(116)

\displaystyle=((1+\frac{\tau}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}})^{2}-1)\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle=((1+\frac{\tau}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}})^{2}-1)\Theta(log{C})

As
\tau
and
\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|
are same order, and
\frac{3\eta}{2M}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}V_{+}^{(t)}=o(\frac{1}{N})
by
V_{+}^{(t)}\leq 2\log{\left(O(\frac{1}{\epsilon})\right)}
,
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
and
\eta\leq\widetilde{O}(\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}})
. Thus, we have

\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle\leq max_{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(t)}\in B({\boldsymbol{X}}^{(t)},\tau)}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

which indicates that the perturbation’s effect exceeds the one-step update on clean data. Therefore,
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle
stay
\Theta(log{C})
. By similar methods, we can bound the other
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(t)}\rangle
, which complete the proof.

H.3
Proof of Claim 11

We use similar methods in
H.2
. We first consider the increment of
\|\boldsymbol{q}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|\boldsymbol{k}\|_{2}^{2}
at the
t
-th update when using the original clean data. We then show that, at this step, the effect introduced by the perturbation under adversarial samples dominates the increment learned from the clean data; hence
\|\boldsymbol{q}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|\boldsymbol{k}\|_{2}^{2}
remains stable and bounded.

By the update rule of
\|\boldsymbol{q}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|\boldsymbol{k}\|_{2}^{2}
we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\langle\Delta\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle+\langle\Delta\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\Delta\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(117)

\displaystyle=2\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)\top}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2|\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle|+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{3\eta}{2M}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\log{\left(O(\frac{1}{\epsilon})\right)}\Theta(\log{C})+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{3\eta}{2NM}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\log{\left(O(\frac{1}{\epsilon})\right)}\Theta(\log{C})

\displaystyle\leq 12\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\log{\left(O(\frac{1}{\epsilon})\right)}\Theta(\log{C})

where the second inequality comes form the upper bounds for
\alpha
and
\beta
on clean data similar to (
114
).

Subsequently, we compute at the
t^{th}
iteration the magnitude of the effect that the perturbation imposes on
\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}
.

\displaystyle max_{\widetilde{X}^{(t)}\in B({X}^{(t)},\tau)}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

(118)

\displaystyle\leq((1+\frac{\tau}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}})^{2}-1)\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq((1+\frac{\tau}{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}})^{2}-1)\Theta(\log{C})

As
\tau
and
\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}
are same order, and
{12\eta}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\log{\left(O(\frac{1}{\epsilon})\right)}=o(\frac{1}{NM})
by
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
and
\eta\leq\widetilde{O}(\min\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{-2},(\sigma_{p}^{2}d)^{-1}\}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}})
. Thus, we have

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq max_{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(t)}\in B({\boldsymbol{X}}^{(t)},\tau)}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

which indicates that the perturbation’s effect exceeds the one-step update on clean data. Therefore,
\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}
stay
\Theta(log{C})
. By similar methods, we can bound the other
\|\boldsymbol{q}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|\boldsymbol{k}\|_{2}^{2}
, which complete the proof.

The proof of convergence and test error is similar with Lemma
G.8
and Section
G.4

Appendix I
Proof of Theorem 3

Proof.

Fix an arbitrary
\theta
. Consider the two classes separately. For the positive class (
y=+
):

\delta_{1}^{(+)}=-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+},\quad\delta_{2}^{(+)}=0

is a valid perturbation since
\|\delta_{1}^{(+)}\|_{2}=\|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+}\|_{2}\leq\tau
,
\|\delta_{2}^{(+)}\|_{2}=0\leq\tau
.
Then the adversarially perturbed point

\widetilde{x}^{(+)}=[\,\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{+},\boldsymbol{\xi}_{2},...,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{M}]=[0,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{2},...,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{M}]

lies in
\mathcal{B}([\boldsymbol{\mu},\boldsymbol{\xi}_{2},...,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{M}],\tau)
, so there exists a perturbation that can potentially flip the classifier’s output for the positive class.

Similarly, for the negative class (
y=-
):

\delta_{1}^{(-)}=-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{-},\quad\delta_{2}^{(-)}=0

produces
\widetilde{x}^{(-)}=[0,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{2},...,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{M}]\in\mathcal{B}([\boldsymbol{\mu},\boldsymbol{\xi}_{2},...,\boldsymbol{\xi}_{M}],\tau)
.

For each class independently, there exists a perturbation that can potentially flip its label.

Thus, at least one class can be adversarially fooled with probability at least
\min\{\Pr[y=+],\Pr[y=-]\}
.
For uniform labels, this gives

L_{D}^{\mathrm{rob}}(\theta)\geq\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{4}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix J
Complete Calculation Process For Benign Overfitting

J.1
Calculations for
\alpha
and
\beta

In this subsection, we give the calculactions for
\alpha
and
\beta
defined in Definition
E.2
.

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{n,i}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta}{NM}\ell_{n}^{\prime(t)}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\cdot\left(-V_{+}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle+V_{n,i}^{(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k\neq i}\left(V_{n,k}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{q}}_{+}^{(t)},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{k}}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right))

Update Rules for Inner Products

In this subsection, we give the update rules for the inner products of
\boldsymbol{q}
and
\boldsymbol{k}
.

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)\top}\right),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{-,-}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,-,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)\top}\right),

We can also derive the update rules for other
\boldsymbol{q}
and
\boldsymbol{k}
, since they follow the same procedure as in Section F.2 of
34
.

for
