\mathcal{A}(t)
:

\displaystyle|\langle q_{\pm}^{(t)},k_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},k_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle q_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle q_{\pm}^{(t)},q_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},q_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle k_{\pm}^{(t)},k_{\mp}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},k_{\pm}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right),

\|q_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|k_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h})

for

.

By Lemma
F.3
we know that
\mathcal{A}(0)
is true. Now we assume
\mathcal{A}(0),\ldots,\mathcal{A}(T)
is true, then we need to prove that
\mathcal{A}(T+1)
is true. We first proof
|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T+1)}\rangle|=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)
, as an example.

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle|=

\displaystyle\left|\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)\top}+\sum_{n\top}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(t)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)\top}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)\cdot\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})

\displaystyle+NM\cdot O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}N}\right)\cdot O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}=O\left(\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)

Taking a summation, we obtain that

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T+1)}\rangle|\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(0)}\rangle|+\sum_{t=0}^{T}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(0)}\rangle|+\sum_{t=0}^{T_{0}-1}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot O(\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{3}{4}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)+O\left(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

Similarly to
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle
, it is easy to know that the inner product does not change by a magnitude more than the product of
\max\{\alpha,\beta\}
and
\max\{\langle q,q\rangle,\langle k,k\rangle\}
in a single iteration, which can be expressed as follows

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle,\boldsymbol{k}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\max\left\{\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}},\frac{\eta(\sigma_{p}^{2}d+\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau)}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}N}\right\}\right)\cdot\Theta(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h},\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)\cdot\Theta(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h},\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\right)

where the second equality is by the condition that
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}=\Omega(1)
.

Taking a summation, we obtain that

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T+1)}\rangle

\displaystyle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(0)}\rangle|\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(t+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T_{0}-1}O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{\eta d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right).

It is clear that the magnitude of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(0)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(0)}\rangle
is smaller than
\max\{\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2},\sigma_{p}^{2}d\}\cdot\sigma_{h}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d_{h}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}
. Thus the magnitude of the bound for
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T+1)}\rangle
is the same as that of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T)}\rangle
. The proof for
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{q}^{(T+1)}\rangle
and
\langle\boldsymbol{k}^{(T+1)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T+1)}\rangle
is exactly the same, and we can conclude the proof by an induction.

J.5
Lower Bounds of
\alpha
and
\beta

In this subsection, we present some bounds for
\alpha
and
\beta
which can be used in
G.2
and
G.3
. All the calculations in this subsection are based on the precise expression for
\alpha
and
\beta
in
J.1
and assume that
\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{B}(s),\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(s-1)
hold (
s\in[T_{1},t]
). Then the following propositions hold:

\displaystyle V_{+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|,

\displaystyle V_{-}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq-3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|,

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle),

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\frac{1}{M}-o(1),

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle),

\displaystyle\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{M}+o(1).

Now we give the bounds respectively for
\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)},\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(s)}
,

\beta_{+,+}^{(s)},\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)},\beta_{-,-}^{(s)},\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)},\beta_{n,i,+}^{(s)},\beta_{n,i,-}^{(s)},\beta_{n,i,n^{\prime},i^{\prime}}^{(s)}
.

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(s)}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(1-\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{2}\cdot V_{+}^{(s)}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(s)}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(1-\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{2}\cdot V_{+}^{(s)}\sum_{k=2}^{M}\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{2NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\ell_{n}^{\prime(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1-\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

where the first inequality is by
V_{+}^{(s)}\geq 3M\cdot|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|
, the second inequality is by the fact that the sum of attention equal to 1 and Lemma
F.4
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{2NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+(M-1)\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+(M-1)\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

(120)

\displaystyle\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{2NM}\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{-}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle)+(M-1)\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle)+(M-1)\exp(\max_{j}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

(121)

Similarly, by applying the update rules in Section
J.3
, we can derive the following bounds on
\alpha
and
\beta
.

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)},\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)},\beta_{+,+}^{(s)},\beta_{-,-}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)},\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)},\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\geq 0,

\displaystyle\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)},\alpha_{n,i,n,j}^{(s)},\beta_{n,i,+}^{(s)},\beta_{n,i,-}^{(s)},\beta_{n,j,n,i}^{(s)}\leq 0.

J.6
Lower Bounds of
\langle\mathbf{q},\mathbf{k}\rangle

In order to give the lower bounds for
\langle q,k\rangle
, we need to rewrite the bounds of
\alpha
and
\beta
in a more concise form. We first expand the equations in
J.5
under the assumption that
\mathcal{B}(s)
and
\mathcal{E}(s)
holds for
s\in[T_{1},t]
.

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{2NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(1-\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\{\text{lower term}\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{2NM}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\{\text{lower term}\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{2NM}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\right)\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta}{2NM}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\right)\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\{\text{lower term}\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{5}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})},

where the first inequality is by Lemma
G.7
(as
\mathcal{E}(s)
holds) and
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\left(\frac{1}{M}-o(1)\right)
. In the fourth inequality, by
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
and the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
(
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle
is increasing and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
is decreasing), there exist a constant
C
such that
C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)
. In the last inequality, we plugging the lower bounds of
V_{+}^{(s)}
and
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}
and then absorb all the constant factors. Similarly, we have

\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{5}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})},

(122)

\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{5}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(s)})},

(123)

\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{5}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,-,j}^{(s)})}.

(124)

With the concise lower bounds for
\alpha
and

above and proposition
\mathcal{C}(s)
, we will give the lower bounds for the dynamics of

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta^{2}C_{5}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{4}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})}\cdot\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})

\displaystyle\quad+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{6}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)_{2}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{w}_{O}}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}(s-T_{1})}{N}\cdot\frac{1}{\exp(\Lambda_{n,+,j}^{(s)})},

(125)

Similarly, we have the lower bounds for the dynamics of other

J.7

In order to give the upper bounds of

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle

(126)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(t)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(t)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(t)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\max_{i=2}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\max_{i=2}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}\cdot(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\max_{i}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\max_{i}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{2}C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{\eta^{2}C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}

where the first inequality is by
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}(s)}\leq 1
and
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)\leq 1
. For the second inequality, we first consider

\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\leq\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}.

Then by the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
we have

\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)\leq C

for
s\in[T_{1},t]
. The last inequality is by
V_{+}^{(s)},V_{n,i}^{(s)}=o(1)
for
s\in[T_{1},t]
and absorbing the constant factors. Similarly, we have

Similar to Section
J.6
, we apply the bounds of
\alpha
and
\beta
above to give the upper bounds for the dynamics
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
.

\displaystyle\langle

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

(127)

\displaystyle+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order terms\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\leq(\frac{\eta^{2}C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{\eta^{2}C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)})\cdot\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})+\text{\lx@text@lbrace lower order terms\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\frac{\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}.

Similarly, we have the upper bounds for the dynamics of other
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
.

J.8
Bounds for the Sum of
\alpha
and
\beta

The gradients of the inner products of
q
and
k
contain a lot of coefficients
\alpha
and
\beta
, and in order to conveniently give the upper bounds of some lower order inner products, we will give upper bounds for the summation of
\alpha
and
\beta
(e.g.
\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|
).

Note that in the Jacobi matrix of the Softmax function, the elements on the diagonal are
\text{softmax}(a_{i})\cdot(1-\text{softmax}(a_{i}))
and the elements on the off-diagonal are
\text{softmax}(a_{i})\cdot\text{softmax}(a_{j})
. In Stage II, the attentions on signals
\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\pm}
increase and the attentions on noises
\boldsymbol{\xi}
decrease, then we can consider the following cases:

•

if
a_{i}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle
or
a_{i}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{i},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}\rangle
,
\text{softmax}(a_{i})
has a constant upper bound 1,
\left(1-\text{softmax}(a_{i})\right)
decreases as
\text{softmax}(a_{i})
increases. So the upper bound of
\text{softmax}(a_{i})\cdot\left(1-\text{softmax}(a_{i})\right)
decreases as
\text{softmax}(a_{i})
increases.

•

if
a_{i}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{j}\rangle
or
a_{i}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{i},\boldsymbol{k}_{j}\rangle
,
\left(1-\text{softmax}(a_{i})\right)
has a constant upper bound 1. So the upper bound of
\text{softmax}(a_{i})\cdot\left(1-\text{softmax}(a_{i})\right)
decreases as
\text{softmax}(a_{i})
decreases.

•

if
a_{j}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+},\boldsymbol{k}_{j}\rangle
or
a_{j}=\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{i},\boldsymbol{k}_{j}\rangle
,
\text{softmax}(a_{i})
has a constant upper bound 1. So the upper bound of
\text{softmax}(a_{i})\cdot\text{softmax}(a_{j})
decreases as
\text{softmax}(a_{j})
decreases.

Based on the above cases, we first study the bounds of the following terms

•

1-\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)

•

1-\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)

•

\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)

•

\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)

Note that
1-\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)=\sum_{j}\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)
and
1-\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)=\sum_{j}\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)
, we only need to give the upper bounds for
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)
and
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)
.

Assume that the propositions
\mathcal{B}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{B}(s),\mathcal{D}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{D}(s-1)
hold
(s\in[T_{1},t])
, we have

|V_{\pm}^{(s)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\leq O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1}),

(128)

\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)\right),

(129)

\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{8}(\sigma_{P}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)\right),

(130)

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in[N],s\in[T_{1},t]
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

(131)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\dfrac{\eta^{2}C_{8}C(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\tfrac{1}{2}}}{N\big(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\big)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{13}+\dfrac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\tfrac{1}{2}}}{N\big(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\big)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}.

For the first inequality, by
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
and the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(s)}\rangle
(
\langle\boldsymbol{q}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}^{(s)}\rangle
is increasing and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
is decreasing), there exists a constant
C
such that

C\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)\geq\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle).
. The second inequality is by plugging (
129
). For the last inequality, by
\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})}=o(1)
, there exist a constant
C_{13}
such that
C_{13}\leq C\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})
and
C_{13}\leq C_{8}C
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}

(132)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,+,j}^{(s)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\sigma_{P}^{2}d+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{1/2}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}.

Plugging above equations into the expressions of
\alpha,\beta
we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

(133)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\cdot 3N}{NM}\cdot\left(O(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}})+\eta C_{4}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})\right)

\displaystyle\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}((\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}\right).

where the third equality is by

\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)\geq 0\text{ for }s\in[T_{1},t].

Next, we give an upper bound for

\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}

as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}(s-T_{1})}{C_{13}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})(s-T_{1}-1)}

(134)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{\frac{C_{13}}{(s-T_{1})}+\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}\cdot(s-T_{1})-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\sqrt{\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}}-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{\frac{2\eta C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)}-\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}{\Theta\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}\right)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

where the inequality is by
ax+\frac{b}{x}\geq 2\sqrt{ab}
for
x>0
, the third equality is by absorbing the lower order term
\frac{\eta^{2}C_{13}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{4}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{2}}
, the last equality is by
\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}=\Theta(1)
. Plugging this into (
133
) and get

\displaystyle|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)+O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)

(135)

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}|=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

(136)

\displaystyle|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,|\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

\displaystyle|\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|=O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}{\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)=

\displaystyle O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\cdot\text{SNR}\cdot N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
.

\displaystyle|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)}|,|\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

(137)

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
, the last equality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
.

\displaystyle|\beta_{n,i,+}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{n,i,-}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta\sigma_{p}^{2}d\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

(138)

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
, the last equality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
.

\displaystyle|\alpha_{n,i,n,j}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{n,j,n,i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)\sigma_{p}d^{\frac{1}{2}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)

(139)

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right),

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in[N]
, the last equality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
.

\displaystyle|\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},j}^{(s)}|,|\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)\sigma_{p}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)

(140)

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{2}\right),

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq n^{\prime}
, the last equality is by
N\cdot\text{SNR}^{2}\geq\Omega(1)
. Taking a summation we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)\cdot O\left(\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)

(141)

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right),

where the last equality is by
\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|=\Theta(1)
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{-,-}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,i,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,i,-}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right),

(142)

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right),

(143)

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\text{SNR}\cdot N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)

(144)

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,+}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,-}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n,j}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,j,n,i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)

(145)

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in S_{+}
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,i,n^{\prime},j}^{(s)}|,\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\beta_{n,j,n^{\prime},i}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)

(146)

for
i,j\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],n\neq n^{\prime}
.

With these sums of
\alpha
and
\beta
above, we can easily prove Claim 3 and Claim 4.

J.9
Proof of Claim 3

In this subsection, we assume that
\mathcal{E}(T_{1}),\ldots,\mathcal{E}(t)
hold, and then proof that
\mathcal{C}(t+1)
is true with the result of
J.8
.

\displaystyle\left|\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\left|\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

(147)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\left|2\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

(148)

\displaystyle+\left.(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)})\cdot(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)\top})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle|+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot O(\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}))+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot O(\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}))

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality. Since
\sigma_{h}^{2}\geq(\max\{\sigma_{p}^{2}d,\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\})^{-1}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-2}
and
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2})
, we have
N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})=o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})
, so
\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}=\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}+o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\|\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)=o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

(149)

\displaystyle\left|\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left((1+\text{SNR})N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)=o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\displaystyle\left|\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)=o(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}d_{n}),

\displaystyle\left|\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)=o(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}d_{n}),

so we have

\displaystyle\|q_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2},\|k_{\pm}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

(150)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h})

for
i\in[M]\setminus\{1\},n\in[N]
.

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t+1)}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|

(151)

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left.\left(\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

(152)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}||\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle|+\sum_{n\in S_{-}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{1}}^{t}|\alpha_{n,-,i}^{(s)}|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|

\displaystyle+O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\cdot o(1)+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\cdot\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)

\displaystyle=|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(T_{1})}\rangle|+O\left(N^{\frac{1}{2}}d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\log(d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the first inequality is triangle inequality, the last equality is by
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2})
. Similarly, we can prove:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\right),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|

\displaystyle,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.

J.10
Upper Bounds of
\langle q,k\rangle

In order to give the upper bounds for
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
in stage III, we need to give the upper bounds of
\alpha
and
\beta
based on the equations in
J.1
. The main difference between this subsection and
J.7
is that the bounds of
|V_{\pm}|,|V_{n,i}|
is
\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)
in this subsection, while the bounds of
|V_{\pm}|,|V_{n,i}|
is
\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)
in
J.7
, resulting in different bounds for
\alpha
and
\beta
. Now we take
\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}
as an example

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

(153)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\max_{i=2}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}

\displaystyle+\max_{i=2}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}\cdot(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\max_{i}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|\tau+\sigma_{p}\tau\sqrt{2\log(4NM/\delta)}+\tau^{2})\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}+\max_{i}|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\cdot\frac{C}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)},

where the first inequality is by
-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime(s)}\leq 1
and
\text{softmax}(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)\leq 1
. For the second inequality, we first consider

\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\leq\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}
,
then by the monotonicity of
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle
and
\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{1})},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(T_{1})}\rangle=o(1)
we have
\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)\leq C
for
t\in[T_{1},T_{3}]
. The last inequality is by
V_{+}^{(s)},|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|\leq 2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)
for
t\in[T_{2},T_{3}]
and absorbing the constant factors. Similar to
J.7
, we can give the bounds for the other
\alpha
and
\beta
as follows:

\displaystyle\alpha_{-,-}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(s)}\rangle)},

(154)

\displaystyle\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}-\tau)^{2}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)},

Similar to
J.6
, we apply the bounds of
\alpha
and
\beta
above to give the upper bounds for the dynamics
\langle q,k\rangle
.

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s+1)}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

(155)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)\top}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\beta_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{+,+}^{(s)}\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\|_{2}^{2}+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta C_{9}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}\cdot\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})+\{\text{lower order term}\}

\displaystyle\leq\eta C_{10}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\frac{1}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)},

Similarly, we have the upper bounds for the dynamics of other
\langle\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{k}\rangle
.

J.11
Bounds for the Sum of
\alpha
and
\beta

Assume that the propositions
\mathcal{F}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{F}(s),\mathcal{H}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{H}(s-1)
hold
(s\in[T_{1},t])
, we have

|V_{\pm}^{(s)}|\leq 2\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right),

(156)

|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|=O(1),

(279)

\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right),

(157)

\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(s)}\geq\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{2})}\geq\log\left(\exp(\Lambda_{n,i,\pm,j}^{(T_{1})})+\Theta\left(\frac{\sigma_{p}^{2}ddd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}\right)\right)

(158)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n\in[N],s\in[T_{2},t]
. Similar to (
59
) and (
60
), we have

\frac{\exp(\langle q_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle q_{\pm}^{(s)},k_{\pm}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle q_{\pm}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

(159)

\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j^{\prime}=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{(s)}\rangle)}=O\left(\frac{N\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}}{\sigma_{p}^{2}dd_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

(160)

Plugging above equations into the expressions of
\alpha,\beta
and letting
O\left(\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\right)
be the upper bound for
|V_{\pm}^{(s)}|,|V_{n,i}^{(s)}|
we have

\displaystyle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{NM}\sum_{n\in S_{+}}-\widetilde{\ell}_{n}^{\prime}(\theta)\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle

(161)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{i=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{M}\langle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{+},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\left(V_{+}^{(s)}\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{M}\left(V_{n,i}^{(s)}\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\frac{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,k}^{(s)}\rangle)}{\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle)+\sum_{j=2}^{M}\exp(\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,j}^{(s)}\rangle)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}}{NM}\cdot\frac{3N}{4}\cdot O\left(\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\right)\cdot O\left(\frac{N\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{3}}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\eta N(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}+\tau)^{2}\left(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta)\right)^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

Taking a summation we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{\eta\epsilon\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot O\left(\eta\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\frac{\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

(162)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right),

where the last equality is by
\|\boldsymbol{w}_{O}\|=\Theta(1)
. Similarly, we have the bounds for other sum of
\alpha
and
\beta
. Thus, we can easily prove Claim 7 and Claim 8.

J.12
Proof of Claim 7

In this subsection, we assume that
\mathcal{I}(T_{2}),\ldots,\mathcal{I}(t)
hold, and then proof that
\mathcal{G}(t+1)
is true with the result of
J.11
.

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\left|\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right|

(163)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\left|2\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\langle\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\cdot\left(\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}+\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{+,+}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(s)}\rangle\right|+2\sum_{n\in S_{+}}\sum_{i=2}^{M}\sum_{s=T_{2}}^{t}\left|\alpha_{n,+,i}^{(s)}\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(s)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(s)}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\{\text{lower order term\lx@text@rbrace}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\cdot\log\left(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+N\cdot M\cdot O\left(\frac{(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\cdot\log\left(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\log\left(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality is by the update rules in
E.2
, the third inequality is by
t\leq T_{3}
. Since
\sigma_{h}^{2}\geq\left(\max\{\sigma_{p}^{2}d,\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\}\right)^{-1}\cdot d_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{-2}
and
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}\left(\max\{\text{SNR}^{4},\text{SNR}^{-4}\}N^{2}\epsilon^{-2}\right)
, we have

\frac{N(\log(6N^{2}M^{2}/\delta))^{3}\log\left(O\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right)\log(\epsilon^{-1}d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}})}{\epsilon d_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}}=o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

(164)

so
\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}=\|\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}+o(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h})
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(\|\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}d_{h}),

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2},\|\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\sigma_{p}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}dd_{h}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{q}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{q}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1),

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{+}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{-}^{(t)}\rangle|,|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{\pm}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)}\rangle|,

\displaystyle|\langle\boldsymbol{k}_{n,i}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{k}_{n^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\rangle|=o(1)

for
i,j\in[M]\backslash\{1\},n,n^{\prime}\in[N],i\neq j\text{ or }n\neq n^{\prime}
.
